# Proposed OEIS comments: A251838 to A255799

One section per OEIS entry: the date the result was obtained, the paper that proves it, what the entry still records as unsettled, and the text proposed for posting on that entry.

**Nothing here has been posted.** See [../SUBMITTING.md](../SUBMITTING.md).

---

## A251838

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A251838>  
Result obtained: **1 September 2026**  
Paper: [5125](../papers/5001-5500/5125-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical: a(n) = 13*a(n-3) +a(n-5) -62*a(n-6) -12*a(n-8) +140*a(n-9) +50*a(n-11) -175*a(n-12) -90*a(n-14) +127*a(n-15) +85*a(n-17) -50*a(n-18) -42*a(n-20) +8*a(n-21) +8*a(n-23) for n>28

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The empirical recurrence is true. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 4 096 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that; checking the one above is then a finite exact computation, and it holds for n > 28. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

> Empirical for row n:

Proposed OEIS comment:

```

> Empirical for row n:

```

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A253396>  
Result obtained: **2 September 2026**  
Paper: [7517](../papers/7501-8000/7517-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical: a(n) = 4*a(n-1) -5*a(n-2) +5*a(n-4) -4*a(n-5) +a(n-6) for n>18
> Empirical for n mod 2 = 0: a(n) = (1/3)*n^4 - (1/3)*n^3 + (337/6)*n^2 - (1423/6)*n + 914 for n>12
> Empirical for n mod 2 = 1: a(n) = (1/3)*n^4 - (1/3)*n^3 + (337/6)*n^2 - (1423/6)*n + 943 for n>12

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The empirical recurrence is true. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 704 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that; checking the one above is then a finite exact computation, and it holds for n > 18. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

Paper: [7470](../papers/7001-7500/7470-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical: a(n) = a(n-2) + 16*a(n-4) - 16*a(n-6) for n>8.
> Empirical g.f.: 8*x*(3026 + 384*x - 2701*x^2 + 84*x^3 - 47804*x^4 - 5578*x^5 + 44239*x^6 + 1600*x^7) / ((1 - x)*(1 + x)*(1 - 2*x)*(1 + 2*x)*(1 + 4*x^2)). - _Colin Barker_, Dec 14 2018

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The empirical recurrence is true. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 24 452 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that; checking the one above is then a finite exact computation, and it holds for n > 8. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical for column k:

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The recurrences in the linked file are correct. Column k counts arrays of a fixed width, so it is a walk count in a finite graph and satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence; Berlekamp-Massey on exact terms returns the minimal one, its order is the order stated for that column, and so is the order of every tail, so any recurrence of that order is this one. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

```

```
